# Government decisions (HG): 2006

1380 acts, 1,506 versions. Each act lives in a folder named by its official identifier, `<kind>/<year>/<number>/` (Legea nr. 31/1990 is `lege/1990/31/`); its README.md lists its articles and the decisions made by hand. Dates are in-force days. Generated on every publish; the same list is in [2006.json](2006.json).

## Withdrawn from the record (1)

Acts published here and later found not to belong (for example, titled as Government decisions but issued by another body). Their folders were removed by a correction commit; the commits that added them stay in the history. Their ids are retired: a later act with the same number gets the id with `-2` appended.

| Act id | Reason | Removed by |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `hg-1898-2006` | published with part of its article text missing: an automatic check was briefly too loose (2026-11-04 18:13-18:24 UTC); withdrawn until it is rebuilt and verified | e74b4fb6fd0 |
